\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \Big (\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} G _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \beta_ {j} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Big) \\ \leq \frac {2 \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta} , \tilde {w}}}{a _ {\Delta}} G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second inequality is derived using Corollary 14. To bound the last term in the above we start from the following observation. Indeed, taking into account definition of  $\beta_{\infty}$  in (20), we get  $(1 - \beta_{\ell}a_{\Delta} / 2)^{-1}\leq 1 + \beta_{\ell}a_{\Delta}$ . This inequality and assumption A2-2 yield that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\gamma_ {\ell - 1}}{\gamma_ {\ell}} \frac {1 - \gamma_ {\ell} a _ {2 2} / 2}{1 - \beta_ {\ell} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \leq (1 + \epsilon_ {\gamma} \gamma_ {\ell}) (1 - \gamma_ {\ell} a _ {2 2} / 2) (1 + \beta_ {\ell} a _ {\Delta}) \tag {52} \\ \leq 1 - \gamma_ {\ell} \left\{a _ {2 2} / 2 - a _ {\Delta} \kappa - \epsilon \right\} + \epsilon_ {\gamma} \gamma_ {\ell} ^ {2} \left\{\kappa a _ {\Delta} - a _ {2 2} / 2 \right\} \leq 1 - (1 / 8) a _ {2 2} \gamma_ {\ell}, \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $\kappa_{\infty} \leq (1/4)a_{22}/a_{\Delta}$ , see (16). We observe the following chain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} = \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \\ = \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \frac {G _ {i + 1 : j - 1} ^ {(2)}}{G _ {i + 1 : j - 1} ^ {(1)}} \stackrel {(a)} {\leq} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j - 1} \prod_ {\ell = i + 1} ^ {j - 1} \frac {\gamma_ {\ell - 1}}{\gamma_ {\ell}} \frac {G _ {i + 1 : j - 1} ^ {(2)}}{G _ {i + 1 : j - 1} ^ {(1)}} \\ \stackrel {(b)} {\leq} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j - 1} \prod_ {\ell = i + 1} ^ {j - 1} (1 - (1 / 8) \gamma_ {\ell} a _ {2 2}) \stackrel {(c)} {\leq} \frac {8 \varsigma}{a _ {2 2}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}. \tag {53} \\ \end{array}
$$

where (a) is due to  $\beta_{j} \leq \beta_{i}$  and  $G_{j+1:k}^{(1)} \leq 1$ , (b) is due to (52), (c) is due to A2-1 and  $\sum_{j=i+1}^{k} \gamma_{j} \prod_{\ell=i+1}^{j-1} (1-\gamma_{\ell}\tilde{a}) \leq (8/a_{22})$  for any  $i, k$ .

Moreover, applying (41), we can bound the last term of (51) as:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \Big (\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} G _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Big) \\ \leq \frac {\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} + \gamma_ {0} \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ \leq \left(\frac {\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} + \gamma_ {0} \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is due to Corollary 14. In addition, similar to (53), we can derive the bound

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \frac {(8 \varsigma) / a _ {2 2}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}
$$

Substituting the above inequalities into (51) leads to

$$
\boxed {\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k + 1} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}} \tag {54}
$$

where

$$
\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} := p _ {\Delta} \Big (\mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \frac {4 \| A _ {1 2} \| \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta} , \tilde {w}}}{a _ {\Delta}} \Big),
$$

$$
\mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} := p _ {\Delta} \left\{\tilde {m} _ {V} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} + 2 \| A _ {1 2} \| \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} + (\| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2} + \tilde {m} _ {V}) \left(\gamma_ {0} \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \frac {\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2}\right) \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \right\},
$$

$$
\mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} := p _ {\Delta} \left\{\frac {1 6 \varsigma \| A _ {1 2} \| \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta} , \tilde {w}}}{a _ {2 2}} + \tilde {m} _ {V} + \left(\| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2} + \tilde {m} _ {V}\right) \frac {8 \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \varsigma / a _ {2 2}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \right\}. \tag {55}
$$

This completes the proof for Proposition 5.

Completing the Proof of Theorem 1 We complete the proof by analyzing the convergence rate of  $\mathrm{M}_k^{\tilde{\theta}}$  using (54). Consider the following recursion which upper bounds  $\mathrm{M}_k^{\tilde{\theta}}$ :

$$
\mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} = \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k + 1} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {U} _ {j},
$$

where we have set  $\mathrm{U}_0 = \mathrm{C}_0^{\tilde{\theta}}$ . Observe that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} - (1 - \beta_ {k} a _ {\Delta} / 2) \mathrm {U} _ {k} = \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} (\beta_ {k + 1} - (1 - \beta_ {k} a _ {\Delta} / 2) \beta_ {k}) + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \gamma_ {k} \beta_ {k} \mathrm {U} _ {k} \\ \Longleftrightarrow \mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} = \big (1 - \beta_ {k} (a _ {\Delta} / 2 - \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \gamma_ {k}) \big) \mathrm {U} _ {k} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} (\beta_ {k + 1} - \beta_ {k} + \beta_ {k} ^ {2} a _ {\Delta} / 2) \\ \end{array}
$$

Since  $\gamma_{k}\leq \gamma_{0}\leq \frac{a_{\Delta}}{4C_{2}^{\vec{\theta}}}$ , we have

$$
\mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} \leq (1 - \beta_ {k} a _ {\Delta} / 4) \mathrm {U} _ {k} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k} ^ {2} a _ {\Delta} / 2
$$

Evaluating the recursion gives

$$
\mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} \leq \prod_ {\ell = 0} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {\ell} a _ {\Delta} / 4) \mathrm {U} _ {0} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} (a _ {\Delta} / 2) \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \prod_ {\ell = j + 1} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {\ell} a _ {\Delta} / 4)
$$

Applying Corollary 14 shows  $\sum_{j=0}^{k} \beta_j^2 \prod_{\ell=j+1}^{k} (1 - \beta_\ell a_\Delta / 4) \leq \varrho^{a_\Delta / 4} \beta_{k+1}$ . Lastly, observing that  $\mathrm{M}_k^\theta \leq \mathrm{U}_k$  gives

$$
\boxed {\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \prod_ {\ell = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \beta_ {\ell} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{4}\right) + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{2} \beta_ {k + 1}.} \tag {56}
$$

To finish the proof of (12), we observe (i) the constant  $\mathrm{C}_0^{\tilde{\theta}}\leq \mathrm{C}_0^{\tilde{\theta},\mathrm{mtg}}\mathrm{V}_0$  for some constant  $\mathrm{C}_0^{\tilde{\theta},\mathrm{mtg}}$ , (ii) the inequality that  $\mathbb{E}[\| \theta_k - \theta^\star \| ^2 ]\leq d_\theta \mathrm{M}_k^{\tilde{\theta}}$ , and (iii) setting the constant  $\mathrm{C}_1^{\tilde{\theta},\mathrm{mtg}}\coloneqq \mathrm{C}_1^{\tilde{\theta}}\varrho^{a_\Delta /4}(a_\Delta /2)$ .

Our last endeavor is to prove (13). Observe that the tracking error  $\widehat{w}_k \coloneqq w_k - A_{22}^{-1}(b_2 - A_{21}\theta_k)$  may be represented as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \widehat {w} _ {k} = w _ {k} - w ^ {\star} + w ^ {\star} - A _ {2 2} ^ {- 1} \left(b _ {2} - A _ {2 1} \theta_ {k}\right) \\ = \tilde {w} _ {k} - C _ {k - 1} \tilde {\theta} _ {k} + A _ {2 2} ^ {- 1} \big ((b _ {2} - A _ {2 1} \theta^ {\star}) - (b _ {2} - A _ {2 1} \theta_ {k}) \big) = \tilde {w} _ {k} - L _ {k} \tilde {\theta} _ {k} \\ \end{array}
$$

using the definitions in (17). This leads to the following estimate of  $\mathrm{M}_k^{\widehat{w}}\coloneqq \| \mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}_k\widehat{w}_k^\top ]\|$ :

$$
\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq 2 \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + 2 \| L _ {k + 1} \| ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq 2 \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + 2 \mathrm {L} _ {\infty} ^ {2} \frac {\lambda_ {\max } (Q _ {2 2})}{\lambda_ {\min } (Q _ {\Delta})} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \tag {57}
$$

In particular, substituting (56) into (41), we obtain:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {k + 1} + \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left\{\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \prod_ {\ell = 0} ^ {j - 1} \left(1 - \beta_ {\ell} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{4}\right) + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{2} \beta_ {j} \right\} \\ \leq \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} + \gamma_ {k + 1} \left\{\mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{2} \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} + \frac {\mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4} \prod_ {\ell = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \beta_ {\ell} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{4}\right) \right\} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is due to the observation  $G_{j + 1:k}^{(2)} \leq \prod_{i = j + 1}^{k}(1 - \gamma_{i}a_{22} / 4)^{2}$  and the application of Corollary 14. Furthermore using  $G_{0:k}^{(2)} \leq \prod_{\ell = 0}^{k}(1 - \beta_{\ell}a_{\Delta} / 4)$  and applying (57) gives

$$
\boxed {\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\widehat {w}} \leq \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {w} \prod_ {\ell = 0} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {\ell} a _ {\Delta} / 4) + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\widehat {w}, \mathrm {m t g}} \gamma_ {k + 1},}
$$

where

$$
\mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {w} := 2 \left\{\mathrm {L} _ {\infty} ^ {2} \frac {\lambda_ {\max } \left(Q _ {2 2}\right)}{\lambda_ {\min } \left(Q _ {\Delta}\right)} \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4} + \mathrm {C} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \right\} \tag {58}
$$

$$
\mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\hat {w}, \mathrm {m t g}} := 2 \Bigl \{\kappa \mathrm {L} _ {\infty} ^ {2} \frac {\lambda_ {\max} (Q _ {2 2})}{\lambda_ {\min} (Q _ {\Delta})} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{2} \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{2} \mathrm {C} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \mathrm {C} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Bigr \}
$$

We conclude the proof for Theorem 1 by observing that  $\mathrm{C}_0^w \leq \mathrm{C}_0^{\widehat{w},\mathrm{mtg}}\mathrm{V}_0$  for some constant  $\mathrm{C}_0^{\widehat{w},\mathrm{mtg}}$ .

# B.2. Detailed Proofs of Theorem 2

To facilitate our discussions next, define a few additional constants as:

$$
\widetilde {G} _ {m: n} ^ {(1)} := \prod_ {i = m} ^ {n} (1 - \beta_ {i} a _ {\Delta} / 4), \quad \widetilde {G} _ {m: n} ^ {(2)} := \prod_ {i = m} ^ {n} (1 - \gamma_ {i} a _ {2 2} / 4)
$$

$$
\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} := \| \Delta \| + \sqrt {\lambda_ {\min} (Q _ {\Delta}) ^ {- 1} \lambda_ {\max} (Q _ {2 2})} \mathrm {L} _ {\infty} \| A _ {1 2} \|, \mathrm {B} _ {2 2, \infty} := \kappa \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \| A _ {1 2} \| + \| A _ {2 2} \|.
$$

Before we begin the proof, notice by observing the form of (25) that that A4 is satisfied by the Markovian noise through setting

$$
m _ {V} = \overline {{\mathrm {b}}} \vee (3 \overline {{\mathrm {A}}}), m _ {W} = \overline {{\mathrm {b}}} \vee (3 \overline {{\mathrm {A}}}),
$$

and furthermore (34) is satisfied with  $\tilde{m}_V, \tilde{m}_W, \tilde{m}_{VW}$  defined in (36) and the above  $m_V, m_W$ . Moreover, for  $i = 0, 1$ , the second order moments of the decomposed noise satisfy:

$$
\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ V _ {k + 1} ^ {(i)} \left(V _ {k + 1} ^ {(i)}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \right\| \leq \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(i)} \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right), \left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ W _ {k + 1} ^ {(i)} \left(W _ {k + 1} ^ {(i)}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \right\| \leq \tilde {m} _ {W} ^ {(i)} \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) (5 9)
$$

$$
\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ V _ {k + 1} ^ {(i)} \left(W _ {k + 1} ^ {(i)}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \right\| \leq \tilde {m} _ {V W} ^ {(i)} \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right), \tag {60}
$$

for some constants  $\tilde{m}_V^{(i)},\tilde{m}_W^{(i)},\tilde{m}_{VW}^{(i)},i = 1,2$  . We proceed with the proof for Theorem 2 as follows.

Bounding  $\mathbf{M}_k^{\tilde{w}}$  (Proof of Lemma 6 and Proposition 7) Repeating the analysis that lead to (40) and using the martingale property of  $V_{k + 1}^{(0)},W_{k + 1}^{(0)}$  shows that

$$
\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} \left(\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \right\| \leq \left(G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}\right) ^ {2} p _ {2 2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \left(G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)}\right) ^ {2} \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}\right), \tag {61}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} = p _ {2 2} \left[ \left\{\mathrm {C} _ {\infty} ^ {2} \vee 1 + \sqrt {d _ {w} d _ {\theta}} \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \right\} \left\{\tilde {m} _ {V} + \kappa^ {2} \tilde {m} _ {W} ^ {(0)} \vee \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {\theta} + \kappa^ {2} \tilde {m} _ {W} ^ {(0)} \right\} \right] \vee \left[ \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} + \kappa^ {2} \tilde {m} _ {W} ^ {(0)} \right]. \tag {62}
$$

Our next endeavor is to bound  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{w}_{k + 1}^{(1)}\|^2 ]$ . Evaluating the recursion in (26) gives

$$
\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} = \Gamma_ {0: k} ^ {(2)} \tilde {w} _ {0} ^ {(1)} + \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(W _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)}\right) \tag {63}
$$

Set  $\widetilde{\psi}_j^{b_i} \coloneqq \psi_j^{b_i} + \Psi_j^{A_{i1}}\theta^\star + \Psi_j^{A_{i2}}w^\star$  for  $i = 1,2$ . Using the definitions, the combined noise has the following expression

$$
\begin{array}{l} W _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)} = \left(\widetilde {\psi} _ {j} ^ {b _ {2}} - \widetilde {\psi} _ {j + 1} ^ {b _ {2}}\right) + C _ {j} \left(\widetilde {\psi} _ {j} ^ {b _ {1}} - \widetilde {\psi} _ {j + 1} ^ {b _ {1}}\right) + \left\{\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {2 2}} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {2 2}} + C _ {j} \left(\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 2}}\right) \right\} \tilde {w} _ {j} \\ + \left\{\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {2 1}} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {2 1}} - (\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {2 2}} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {2 2}}) C _ {j - 1} + C _ {j} (\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 1}}) - C _ {j} (\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 2}}) C _ {j - 1} \right\} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \\ \end{array}
$$

Upon some algebra manipulations that are detailed in Appendix B.2.1, we deduce that the combined noise may be decomposed as:

$$
\begin{array}{l} W _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)} \equiv \psi_ {j} ^ {W V} - \psi_ {j + 1} ^ {W V} + \widetilde {\Psi} _ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} + \widetilde {\Psi} _ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {j} \\ + \left(\Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} - \Upsilon_ {j + 1} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1}\right) + \left(\Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {j} - \Upsilon_ {j + 1} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1}\right) \tag {63} \\ + \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \big (\tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \big) + \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \big (\tilde {w} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {w} _ {j} \big), \\ \end{array}
$$

where it holds that

$$
\| \psi_ {j} ^ {W V} \| \vee \| \Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \| \vee \| \Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \| \vee \| \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \| \vee \| \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \| \leq \mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}, \| \widetilde {\Psi} _ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \| \vee \| \widetilde {\Psi} _ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \| \leq \mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V} \gamma_ {j},
$$

with

$$
\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V} := \max  \left\{\overline {{\mathrm {b}}} \left(1 + \mathrm {C} _ {\infty}\right), \overline {{\mathrm {A}}} \left(1 + 2 \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} + \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} ^ {2}\right), \overline {{\mathrm {A}}} C _ {2} ^ {U} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \left(1 + \mathrm {C} _ {\infty}\right) (1 + \varsigma) \right\}. \tag {64}
$$

Let us bound the second term in (63) one by one as follows. Using Lemma 11, we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \Big (\psi_ {j} ^ {W V} - \psi_ {j + 1} ^ {W V} + \left(\Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} - \Upsilon_ {j + 1} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1}\right) + \left(\Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {j} - \Upsilon_ {j + 1} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1}\right) \Big) \\ = \gamma_ {0} \Gamma_ {1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\psi_ {0} ^ {W V} + \Upsilon_ {0} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {0} + \Upsilon_ {0} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {0}\right) - \gamma_ {k} \left(\psi_ {k + 1} ^ {W V} + \Upsilon_ {k + 1} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} + \Upsilon_ {k + 1} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {k + 1}\right) \\ + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k} \big (\gamma_ {j} ^ {2} B _ {2 2} ^ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} + (\gamma_ {j} - \gamma_ {j - 1}) \Gamma_ {j: k} ^ {(2)} \big) \big (\psi_ {j} ^ {W V} + \Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} + \Upsilon_ {j} ^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \tilde {w} _ {j} \big), \\ \end{array}
$$

Secondly,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \big (\tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \big) = - \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \beta_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {\theta}} \big (A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1} + V _ {j + 1} \big) \\ \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \big (\tilde {w} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {w} _ {j} \big) = - \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \Phi^ {W V, \tilde {w}} \big (W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} \big) \\ \end{array}
$$

As a consequence of (59)-(60), we have

$$
\mathbb {E} [ \| A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1} + V _ {j + 1} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} \big (1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \big), \quad \mathbb {E} [ \| W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}} \big (1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \big)
$$

where

$$
\tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} := 2 \big \{\| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2} + \tilde {m} _ {V} \big \}, \quad \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}} := 2 (\tilde {m} _ {W} + \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \tilde {m} _ {V}).
$$

Noting that  $\tilde{w}_0^{(1)} = 0$ , taking Euclidean norm on both sides of (63) yields

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| \leq \sqrt {p _ {2 2}} \Big \{\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V} \left[ G _ {1: k} ^ {(2)} \gamma_ {0} (1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \|) + \gamma_ {k} (1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} \| + \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} \|) \right] \Big \} \\ + \sqrt {p _ {2 2}} \mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V} \left\{\sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\gamma_ {j} ^ {2} + \left\| \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} B _ {2 2} ^ {j} + \left(\gamma_ {j} - \gamma_ {j - 1}\right) \left(\mathrm {I} - B _ {2 2} ^ {j}\right) \right\|\right) \left(1 + \left\| \tilde {w} _ {j} \right\| + \left\| \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \right\|\right) \right\} \tag {65} \\ + \sqrt {p _ {2 2}} \mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (\kappa \| A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1} + V _ {j + 1} \| + \| W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} \|) \\ \end{array}
$$

Note that for any sequence  $(b_{j})_{j\geq 0}$ , the following inequality holds:

$$
\left(\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} b _ {j}\right) ^ {2} \leq \left(\sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(2)}\right) \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} b _ {j} ^ {2} \leq \gamma_ {k + 1} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} b _ {j} ^ {2}, \tag {66}
$$

where the first inequality is due to Jensen's inequality and the second inequality is due to Corollary 14. Using  $\| B_{22}^{j}\| \leq \mathrm{B}_{22,\infty},|\gamma_{j} - \gamma_{j - 1}|\leq \frac{a_{22}}{8}\gamma_{j}^{2}$  [cf. it is a direct consequence of A2-2 and the

fact  $\gamma_{j}\leq \gamma_{j - 1}]$  and applying the above inequality to (65) yields

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} \leq 9 p _ {2 2} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}) ^ {2} \left\{\left(G _ {1: k} ^ {(2)}\right) ^ {2} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} \left(1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|\right) ^ {2} + \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \left(1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} \| ^ {2} + \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} \| ^ {2}\right) \right\} \\ + 9 p _ {2 2} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}) ^ {2} (\mathrm {B} _ {2 2, \infty} + \frac {a _ {2 2}}{8} + 1) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} \Bigl \{\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {j} \| ^ {2} + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \| ^ {2}) \Bigr \} \\ + 9 p _ {2 2} \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}\right) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\kappa \| A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1} + V _ {j + 1} \| ^ {2} + \| W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} \| ^ {2}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

Using the fact  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{w}_k\|^2 ]\leq d_w\| \mathbb{E}[\tilde{w}_k\tilde{w}_k^\top ]\|$ ,  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{\theta}_k\|^2 ]\leq d_\theta \| \mathbb{E}[\tilde{\theta}_k\tilde{\theta}_k^\top ]\|$  (cf. Corollary 22), taking the expectation on both sides yields

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq 9 p _ {2 2} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}) ^ {2} \Big \{(G _ {1: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} (1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|) ^ {2} + \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} (1 + d _ {\theta} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + d _ {w} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}}) \Big \} \\ + 9 p _ {2 2} \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}\right) ^ {2} \left(\mathrm {B} _ {2 2, \infty} + \frac {a _ {2 2}}{8} + 1\right) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} \left\{\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(1 + d _ {\theta} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + d _ {w} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) \right\} \\ + 9 p _ {2 2} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}) ^ {2} (\kappa \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} + \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}}) \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \big (1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \big) \\ \end{array}
$$

The above simplifies to

$$
\mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} \left(G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}\right) ^ {2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \left(\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \tag {66}
$$

where we have used  $\gamma_{k + 1}\leq \gamma_k$  and defined

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} = 9 p _ {2 2} \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}\right) ^ {2} \left(1 + \left\| \tilde {w} _ {0} \right\| + \left\| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \right\|\right) ^ {2} \left(\gamma_ {0} / \left(1 - \gamma_ {0} a _ {2 2} / 2\right)\right) ^ {2},
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} = 9 p _ {2 2} \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}\right) ^ {2} \left(d _ {\theta} \vee d _ {w}\right),
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} = 9 p _ {2 2} \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {W V}\right) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \left[ \left(d _ {\theta} \vee d _ {w}\right) \left(\mathrm {B} _ {2 2, \infty} + \frac {a _ {2 2}}{8} + 1\right) ^ {2} + \left(\kappa \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} + \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}}\right) \right], \tag {67}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4} = \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} + \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3}.
$$

Notice that the intermediate results (61), (66) lead to Lemma 6.

Compared to (61), an important feature of the bound (66) is that the latter contains an extra  $\gamma_{k}$  factor. This indicates that the iterate  $\tilde{w}_{k + 1}^{(1)}$  driven by Markovian noise decays at a faster rate. As we will demonstrate below, the effect of the additional Markov noise is thus negligible compared to the martingale noise driven terms.

As the operator norm  $\| \cdot \|$  is convex, applying Jensen's inequality yields

$$
\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq 2 \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} (\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)}) ^ {\top} ] \| + 2 \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \leq 2 \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} ] + 2 \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \|
$$

Substituting (61) and (66) gives

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq 2 \Bigl \{\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} (G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} (\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (\mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \Bigr \} \\ + 2 \Bigl \{p _ {2 2} (G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (\mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) \Bigr \} \\ \end{array}
$$

The assumption on step size in (27) guarantees  $2\widetilde{C}_2\gamma_k^2 \leq (1/2)$ , which further implies

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq 4 \Bigl \{\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} (G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (\mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \Bigr \} \\ + 4 \Bigl \{p _ {2 2} (G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (\mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) \Bigr \} \\ \end{array}
$$

Like in the proof of Theorem 1, we set

$$
\mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} = G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} + p _ {2 2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}}) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1} + \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {j} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \big) (\mathrm {U} _ {j} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}})
$$

with  $\mathrm{U}_0 = \widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_1 + p_{22}\mathrm{M}_0^{\tilde{w}}$ . Through evaluating the recursion, we observe that for any  $k\geq 0$ , it holds

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq 4 \left\{\mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k + 1} \gamma_ {j - 1} ^ {2} G _ {j: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4}\right) \right\} \tag {66} \\ \leq 4 \Big \{\mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} + \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4}) + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4}) \Big \} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is due to A2-2 which guarantees that  $\gamma_{j - 1}^2 G_{j:k}^{(2)}\leq \gamma_j^2 G_{j + 1:k}$ . Moreover, the sequence  $\mathrm{U}_{k + 1}$  can be expressed as follows:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} - (1 - \gamma_ {k} a _ {2 2} / 2) \mathrm {U} _ {k} = \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (\gamma_ {k + 1} - \gamma_ {k} (1 - \gamma_ {k} a _ {2 2} / 2)) + \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \big) (\mathrm {U} _ {k} + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) \\ \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (a _ {2 2} / 2) \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} + \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0}\right) \left(\mathrm {U} _ {k} + \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

As the step size satisfies  $\gamma_k\left(\widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_3\gamma_0 + \widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_0\right) \leq \frac{a_{22}}{4}$ , we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} \leq (1 - \gamma_ {k} a _ {2 2} / 4) \mathrm {U} _ {k} + \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {0} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0}\right) \mathrm {M} _ {k} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (a _ {2 2} / 2) \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \\ \Rightarrow \mathrm {U} _ {k + 1} \leq \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {U} _ {0} + \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \Big \{\big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {0} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \big) \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (a _ {2 2} / 2)) \Big \}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Substituting the above into (66) yields

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq 4 \Bigl \{\widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {U} _ {0} + \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \bigl \{\bigl (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {0} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \bigr) \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (a _ {2 2} / 2)) \bigr \} \Bigr \} \\ + 4 \left\{\gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4}\right) + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4}\right) \right\} \\ \end{array}
$$

Finally, using the fact that  $\gamma_k^2 \leq \varsigma \gamma_{k+1}$  yields

$$
\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}. \tag {66}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} := 4 \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} + p _ {2 2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right), \quad \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} := 4 \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {4} \left(\varsigma + \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2}\right) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \left(\varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2}\right) ^ {2} \left(a _ {2 2} / 2\right)\right) \tag {67}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} := 4 \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {0} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0}\right), \quad \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} := 4 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2}.
$$

This concludes the proof for Proposition 7.

Before we proceed, we need to bound  $\| \mathbb{E}[\tilde{w}_{k + 1}^{(0)}(\tilde{w}_{k + 1}^{(0)})^\top ]$  and  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{w}_{k + 1}^{(1)}\| ^2 ]$  as well. Substituting (66) into (61) yields

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \leq (G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} p _ {2 2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} (1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}) \tag {68} \\ + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} \Big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j - 1} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Big) \\ \end{array}
$$

We observe

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} = \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)}) ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)}
$$

$$
\stackrel {(a)} {\leq} \frac {1}{1 - \gamma_ {0} a _ {2 2} / 4} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \stackrel {(b)} {\leq} \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \gamma_ {k + 1}}{1 - \gamma_ {0} a _ {2 2} / 4} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(2)}
$$

where (a) is due to  $G_{j+1:k}^{(2)} \leq \widetilde{G}_{j+1:k}^{(2)}$  and (b) is due to Corollary 14. As such, combining terms in (68) yields:

$$
\left|\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} \left(\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}\right) ^ {\top} \right]\right\| \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \gamma_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \right.\left. \right. \tag {69}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} := p _ {2 2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \quad \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} := \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \left(1 + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right)
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} := \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} \Big (1 + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} \frac {\gamma_ {0}}{1 - \gamma_ {0} a _ {2 2} / 4} \Big)
$$

Similarly, we can compute the bound for  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{w}_{k + 1}^{(1)}\|^2 ]$  as follows. Using (66):

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} \left(G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)}\right) ^ {2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \tag {70} \\ + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \\ \end{array}
$$

Notice that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j - 1} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}\right) \\ \leq \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0}) \gamma_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \\ \end{array}
$$

Since  $(1 - \gamma a_{22} / 2) \leq (1 - \gamma a_{22} / 4)^{2}$  for any  $\gamma > 0$ , we have  $G_{j + 1:k}^{(2)} \leq (\widetilde{G}_{j + 1:k}^{(2)})^2$ , therefore together with Corollary 14 it yields

$$
\sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \leq \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 4} \gamma_ {k + 1}}{1 - \gamma_ {0} a _ {2 2} / 4} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(2)}.
$$

Collecting terms and substituting them in (70) yield

$$
\mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \gamma_ {k + 1} ^ {2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \gamma_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \tag {70}
$$

where we use again the fact that  $\gamma_k^2\leq \varsigma \gamma_{k + 1}$  and

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} := \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} + \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}}, \quad \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} := \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 2} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0}) + \varsigma \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} := \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} \Big (1 + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 4} \gamma_ {0}}{1 - \gamma_ {0} a _ {2 2} / 4} \Big) + \varsigma \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}}, \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} := \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} (1 + \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}}).
$$

Bounding the Cross Term (Proof of Lemma 8) Our next endeavor is to bound the cross variance between the martingale noise driven terms  $\tilde{w}_{k+1}^{(0)}$  and  $\tilde{\theta}_{k+1}^{(0)}$ . Here, the steps involved are similar to those in bounding  $\mathbf{M}_k^{\tilde{\theta},\tilde{w}}$  in the proof of Theorem 1. Particularly, in a similar vein as the derivation of (43), we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \leq p _ {2 2, \Delta} \Big \{G _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} + \| A _ {1 2} \| \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(2)} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \Big \} \\ + p _ {2 2, \Delta} \Big \{\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \| \mathbb {E} [ V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)} (W _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| + \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \| \mathbb {E} [ V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)} (V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \Big \} \\ \end{array}
$$

By observing that  $G_{j:k}^{(2)} \leq \widetilde{G}_{j:k}^{(2)}$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \mathbb {E} [ \widetilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \leq p _ {2 2, \Delta} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} + p _ {2 2, \Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(2)} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \\ + p _ {2 2, \Delta} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \left(\tilde {m} _ {V W} ^ {(0)} \gamma_ {j} + \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \beta_ {j}\right) \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) \tag {71} \\ \end{array}
$$

When combined with (66), (69), it can be verified using similar steps as in deriving (44) that:

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(2)} | | \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] | | \leq \frac {2 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \widetilde {G} _ {0 : k} ^ {(2)}}{a _ {\Delta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 4} \beta_ {k + 1} + \frac {2 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}}}{a _ {\Delta}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}},
$$

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \frac {2 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0 : k} ^ {(2)}}{a _ {\Delta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 4} \beta_ {k + 1} + \left(\frac {2 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}}}{a _ {\Delta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}},
$$

Substituting the above into (71) gives:

$$
\left|\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} \left(\tilde {w} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}\right) ^ {\top} \right]\right\| \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \right.\left. \right. \tag {72}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} := p _ {2 2, \Delta} \Big (\mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} + \frac {\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \| A _ {1 2} \| + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \left(\tilde {m} _ {V W} ^ {(0)} \gamma_ {0} + \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \beta_ {0}\right)}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Big)
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} := p _ {2 2, \Delta} \varrho^ {a _ {2 2} / 4} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \| A _ {1 2} \| + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \left(\tilde {m} _ {V W} ^ {(0)} \gamma_ {0} + \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \beta_ {0}\right)\right) \tag {73}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} := p _ {2 2, \Delta} \Bigl \{\frac {2 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}}}{a _ {\Delta}} \| A _ {1 2} \| + \Bigl (\frac {2 \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}}}{a _ {\Delta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \Bigr) \bigl (\tilde {m} _ {V W} ^ {(0)} \gamma_ {0} + \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \mathrm {C} _ {\infty} \beta_ {0} \bigr) \Bigr \}
$$

Notice that this concludes the proof of Lemma 8.

Bounding  $\mathrm{M}_k^\theta$  (Proof of Proposition 9) Like in the proof of Theorem 1, we begin by bounding  $\| \mathbb{E}[\tilde{w}_k^{(0)}(\tilde{w}_k^{(0)})^\top ]\|$  as follows. Evaluating the recursion in (26) and following the derivations that lead to (50), we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \mathbb {E} \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \leq p _ {\Delta} \left\{\left(G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + 2 \| A _ {1 2} \| \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {\theta} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \right\} \\ + p _ {\Delta} \Bigl \{\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \big (\| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| + \| \mathbb {E} [ V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)} (V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \Bigr) \Bigr \} \\ \end{array}
$$

We apply (72) and note that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} | | \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {\theta} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] | | \\ \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} \Big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \beta_ {j} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Big) \\ \stackrel {(a)} {\leq} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \frac {G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ \end{array}
$$

where (a) is due to the observation that  $1 - \gamma_j a_{22} / 4 \leq 1 - \beta_j a_\Delta / 2$  and the application of Lemma 12. Moreover, by a slight modification of (53), we have

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \frac {1 6 \varsigma}{a _ {2 2}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \tag {73}
$$

Therefore,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} G _ {j: k} ^ {(1)} | | \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {\theta} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] | | \\ \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \frac {G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \frac {1 6 \varsigma}{a _ {2 2}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ \end{array}
$$

Similarly, we apply (69), (73) and note that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| \\ \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \left(G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}\right) ^ {2} \left\{\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \gamma_ {j} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \right\} \tag {74} \\ \leq (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \gamma_ {0}) \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \frac {\beta_ {0} (1 6 \varsigma / a _ {2 2})}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ \end{array}
$$

Finally, we obtain that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \left(G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}\right) ^ {2} \| \mathbb {E} \left[ V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)} \left(V _ {j + 1} ^ {(0)}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \| \leq \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \left(G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}\right) ^ {2} \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) \\ \leq \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \left\{\varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \right\} \\ \end{array}
$$

Using the bound in (66) and the derivations in (74), we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0}) \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \frac {\beta_ {0} (1 6 \varsigma / a _ {2 2})}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} \beta_ {0} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {i} \gamma_ {i} G _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \\ \end{array}
$$

Combining the above results, we obtain that

$$
\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} \left(\tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \right\| \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(0)} G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(0)} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(0)} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \tag {75}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(0)} = p _ {\Delta} \Big (\mathrm {M} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} \frac {4}{a _ {\Delta} / 4} \| A _ {1 2} \| \Big),
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(0)} = p _ {\Delta} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Big (2 \| A _ {1 2} \| \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}, \tilde {w}} + \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime}} \gamma_ {0}) + \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0}) \Big),
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(0)} = p _ {\Delta} \Big \{2 \| A _ {1 2} \| \frac {\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta} , \tilde {w}}}{a _ {2 2}} + \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2} \frac {\beta_ {0} (1 6 \varsigma / a _ {2 2})}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} + \tilde {m} _ {V} ^ {(0)} \Big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \frac {\beta_ {0} (1 6 \varsigma / a _ {2 2})}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} \beta_ {0} \Big) \Big \}
$$

To bound the term  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{\theta}_{k + 1}^{(1)}\|^2]$ , we proceed by considering the following decomposition:

$$
\tilde{\theta}_{k + 1}^{(1)} = \underbrace{\Gamma_{0:k}^{(1)}\tilde{\theta}_{0}^{(1)} + \sum_{j = 0}^{k}\beta_{j}\Gamma_{j + 1:k}^{(1)}A_{12}\tilde{w}_{j}^{(1)}}_{= \tilde{\theta}_{k + 1}^{(1,0)}} + \underbrace{\sum_{j = 0}^{k}\beta_{j}\Gamma_{j + 1:k}^{(1)}V_{j + 1}^{(1)}}_{= \tilde{\theta}_{k + 1}^{(1,1)}}
$$

As  $\tilde{\theta}_0^{(1)} = 0$ , we observe that

$$
\| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 0)} \| \leq \sqrt {p _ {\Delta}} \| A _ {1 2} \| \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \| \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(1)} \|
$$

Taking square on both sides and applying the Jensen's inequality (66) yields

$$
\mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 0)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \frac {p _ {\Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {w} _ {j} ^ {(1)} \| ^ {2} ]
$$

Applying (70) gives

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 0)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \frac {p _ {\Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \gamma_ {j - 1} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2}\right) \\ + \frac {p _ {\Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\varrho}} \tag {76} \\ \end{array}
$$

Let us bound the right hand side one by one, we observe

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} (\widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \leq \frac {G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \leq \frac {(4 / a _ {\Delta}) G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2}. \\ \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \gamma_ {j - 1} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \rho_ {0} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \leq \rho_ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last two inequalities are due to  $\gamma_{j - 1}^2\leq \rho_0\beta_j$  , see B4. In addition, using the fact  $G_{m:n}^{(1)}\leq$ $(\widetilde{G}_{m:n}^{(1)})^2$  , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} = \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \\ \leq \rho_ {0} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} (\widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \leq \frac {\rho_ {0}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \\ \leq \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \rho_ {0}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\vartheta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \\ \end{array}
$$

Substituting these back into (76) yields

$$
\mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 0)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 0)} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 0)} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 0)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)}, \tag {77}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 0)} = \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\bar {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \frac {8 p _ {\Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2}}{a _ {\Delta} ^ {2} (1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2)}, \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 0)} = \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\bar {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \frac {p _ {\Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} \rho_ {0} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2},
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 0)} = \frac {p _ {\Delta} \| A _ {1 2} \| ^ {2}}{a _ {\Delta} / 2} \rho_ {0} \Big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w} ^ {\prime \prime}} \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4} \Big).
$$

Next, we bound  $\mathbb{E}[\| \tilde{\theta}_k^{(1,1)}\|^2]$ . Set  $\widetilde{\psi}_j^{b_1} \coloneqq \psi_j^{b_1} + \Psi_j^{A_{11}}\theta^\star + \Psi_j^{A_{12}}w^\star$ , upon some algebraic manipulations (details in Appendix B.2.1) we observe the following decomposition

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {j + 1} ^ {(1)} \equiv \widetilde {\psi} _ {j} ^ {b _ {1}} - \widetilde {\psi} _ {j + 1} ^ {b _ {1}} + \left(\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1}\right) + \left(\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {j} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1}\right) \tag {78} \\ + \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \left(\tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {\theta} _ {j}\right) + \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \left(\tilde {w} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {w} _ {j}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

and from B3 we have

$$
\| \widetilde {\psi} _ {j} ^ {b _ {1}} \| \vee \| \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \| \vee \| \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \| \leq \mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V} := \overline {{\mathrm {A}}} \vee (\overline {{\mathrm {b}}} + \overline {{\mathrm {A}}} (\| \theta^ {\star} \| + \| w ^ {\star} \|)).
$$

Applying Lemma 11, we can show

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \left(\widetilde {\psi} _ {j} ^ {b _ {1}} - \widetilde {\psi} _ {j + 1} ^ {b _ {1}} + \left(\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1}\right) + \left(\Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {j} - \Psi_ {j + 1} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {j + 1}\right)\right) \\ = \beta_ {0} \Gamma_ {1: k} ^ {(1)} \left(\widetilde {\psi} _ {0} ^ {b _ {1}} + \Psi_ {0} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {0} + \Psi_ {0} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {0}\right) - \beta_ {k} \left(\widetilde {\psi} _ {k + 1} ^ {b _ {1}} + \Psi_ {k + 1} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} + \Psi_ {k + 1} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {k + 1}\right) \\ + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k} \big (\beta_ {j} ^ {2} B _ {1 1} ^ {k} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} + (\beta_ {j} - \beta_ {j - 1}) \Gamma_ {j: k} ^ {(1)} \big) \big (\widetilde {\psi} _ {j} ^ {b _ {1}} + \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} \tilde {\theta} _ {j} + \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \tilde {w} _ {j} \big). \\ \end{array}
$$

Moreover,

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} (\tilde {\theta} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {\theta} _ {j}) = - \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 1}} (A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j} + W _ {j + 1})
$$

$$
\sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} \big (\tilde {w} _ {j + 1} - \tilde {w} _ {j} \big) = - \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} \Gamma_ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \Psi_ {j} ^ {A _ {1 2}} (W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1})
$$

The above inequalities allow us to upper bound  $\| \tilde{\theta}_{k + 1}^{(1,1)}\|$ . Note that as  $|\beta_{j} - \beta_{j - 1}|\leq \frac{a_{\Delta}}{16}\beta_{j}^{2}$  [cf. A2], we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 1)} \| \leq \sqrt {p _ {\Delta}} \operatorname {E} _ {0} ^ {V} \left\{\frac {G _ {0 : k} ^ {(1)}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} (1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|) + \beta_ {k} (1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} \| + \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} \|) \right\} \\ + \sqrt {p _ {\Delta}} \mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V} (\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} + a _ {\Delta} / 1 6) \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \big (1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \| + \| \tilde {w} _ {j} \| \big) \\ + \sqrt {p _ {\Delta}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \big (\beta_ {j} ^ {2} \| A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j} + W _ {j + 1} \| + \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} \| W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} \| \big), \\ \end{array}
$$

Applying the Jensen's inequality (66) and taking square on both sides give

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 1)} \| ^ {2} \leq 7 p _ {\Delta} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V}) ^ {2} \Big \{(G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \Big (\frac {1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Big) ^ {2} + \beta_ {k} ^ {2} (1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} \| ^ {2} + \| \tilde {w} _ {k + 1} \| ^ {2}) \Big \} \\ + 7 p _ {\Delta} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V}) ^ {2} (\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} + a _ {\Delta} / 1 6) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} (1 + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {j} \| ^ {2} + \| \tilde {w} _ {j} \| ^ {2}) \Big \} \\ + 7 p _ {\Delta} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Bigl \{\beta_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \| A _ {1 2} \tilde {w} _ {j} + W _ {j + 1} \| ^ {2} + \gamma_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} \| W _ {j + 1} + C _ {j} V _ {j + 1} \| ^ {2} \Bigr \}, \\ \end{array}
$$

Note the subtle difference that the last term takes  $\gamma_{k + 1}$ . Taking expectation on both sides leads to

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq 7 p _ {\Delta} \Big \{(G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \Big (\frac {1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Big) ^ {2} + \beta_ {k} ^ {2} (1 + d _ {\theta} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + d _ {w} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}}) \Big \} \\ + 7 p _ {\Delta} (\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V}) ^ {2} (\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} + a _ {\Delta} / 1 6) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} (1 + d _ {\theta} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + d _ {w} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}) \Bigg \} \\ + 7 p _ {\Delta} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} (\beta_ {0} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} + \beta_ {j} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}}) (1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}), \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have used  $\beta_{k + 1}\leq \beta_0$  and  $\gamma_{k + 1}\leq \gamma_j$ . Again, using the bound  $\gamma_j^2\leq \rho_0\beta_j$  from B4, we can simplify the above inequality into

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq 7 p _ {\Delta} \Big \{(G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \Big (\frac {1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Big) ^ {2} + \beta_ {k} ^ {2} (1 + d _ {\theta} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + d _ {w} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}}) \Big \} \\ \left. + 7 p _ {\Delta} \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V}\right) ^ {2} \left(\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} + a _ {\Delta} / 1 6\right) ^ {2} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \left(1 + d _ {\theta} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + d _ {w} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) \right\} \\ + 7 p _ {\Delta} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} (\beta_ {0} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} + \rho_ {0} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}}) \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} (1 + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}) \\ \leq \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)} \left(G _ {0: k} ^ {(1)}\right) ^ {2} + \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} \beta_ {k} ^ {2} \left(1 + \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) + \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \left(\mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}}\right) + \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {(1, 1)} \beta_ {k + 1}, \tag {76} \\ \end{array}
$$

where

$$
\widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)} = 7 p _ {\Delta} \Big (\frac {1 + \| \tilde {w} _ {0} \| + \| \tilde {\theta} _ {0} \|}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 2} \Big) ^ {2}, \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} = 7 p _ {\Delta} (d _ {\theta} \vee d _ {w})
$$

$$
\widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} = 7 p _ {\Delta} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \left\{\left(d _ {\theta} \vee d _ {w}\right) \left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V}\right) ^ {2} \left(\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} + a _ {\Delta} / 1 6\right) ^ {2} \beta_ {0} + \left(\beta_ {0} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} + \rho_ {0} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}}\right) \right\}
$$

$$
\widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {(1, 1)} = 7 p _ {\Delta} \left(\varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2}\right) ^ {2} \left(\left(\mathrm {E} _ {0} ^ {V}\right) ^ {2} \left(\mathrm {B} _ {1 1, \infty} + a _ {\Delta} / 1 6\right) ^ {2} + \beta_ {0} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {\theta}} + \rho_ {0} \tilde {m} _ {\Delta \tilde {w}}\right)
$$

Observe that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \Bigl \{\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: j - 1} ^ {(2)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {j - 1} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Bigr \} \\ \leq \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0}\right) \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Furthermore, using  $G_{j+1:k}^{(1)} \leq (\widetilde{G}_{j+1:k}^{(1)})^2$  and  $\widetilde{G}_{i+1:j-1}^{(2)} \leq \widetilde{G}_{i+1:j-1}^{(1)}$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {j - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} = \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} G _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(2)} \\ \leq \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {j = i + 1} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} (\widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)}) ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: j - 1} ^ {(1)} \leq \frac {\varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \beta_ {k + 1}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \\ \end{array}
$$

Moreover, through applying  $\widetilde{G}_{i+1:j-1}^{(2)} \leq \widetilde{G}_{i+1:j-1}^{(1)}$  and  $\beta_k \leq \beta_j$  for any  $j \leq k$ , we have

$$
\beta_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \leq \beta_ {0} ^ {2} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \varsigma \gamma_ {0} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}},
$$

where we have used  $\beta_k^2 \leq \varsigma \beta_{k+1}$ . The above results simplify (76) into

$$
\mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 1)} \| ^ {2} ] \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {j + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {j} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {(1, 1)} \beta_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}, \tag {76}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathbf {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)} = \widehat {\mathbf {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)} + \beta_ {0} ^ {2} \widehat {\mathbf {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} \widetilde {\mathbf {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}},
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} = \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} (1 + \varsigma \gamma_ {0} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}}) + \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {(1, 1)} + \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0}) \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 2}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} = \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} \left(1 + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 4} \beta_ {0}}{1 - \beta_ {0} a _ {\Delta} / 4}\right) + \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2}, \tag {77}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {(1, 1)} = \widehat {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)} (1 + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {\tilde {w}} \gamma_ {0} ^ {2}).
$$

Finally, combining (75), (77), (76) gives

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq 3 \left(\| \mathbb {E} [ \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)} (\tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(0)}) ^ {\top} ] \| + \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 0)} \| ^ {2} ] + \mathbb {E} [ \| \tilde {\theta} _ {k + 1} ^ {(1, 1)} \| ^ {2} ]\right) \\ \leq 3 \left\{\left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)}\right) \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)}\right) \beta_ {k + 1} \right\} \\ + 3 \Bigl \{\bigl (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} \bigr) \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {3} ^ {(1, 1)} \beta_ {k} ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \Bigr \} \\ \end{array}
$$

As we have  $3\widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_3^{(1,1)}\beta_k^2\leq 1 / 2$  , we have

$$
\boxed {\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k + 1} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \mathrm {M} _ {i} ^ {\tilde {\theta}},}
$$

where

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} := 6 \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {(1, 1)}\right), \quad \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} := 6 \left(\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {(1, 1)}\right), \tag {78}
$$

$$
\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} := 6 \big (\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 0)} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {(1, 1)} \big).
$$

This concludes the proof of Proposition 9.

Completing the Proof of Theorem 2 From (30) we can derive a bound for  $\mathrm{M}_k^\theta$  as follows. Let  $\tilde{\mathrm{U}}_0 = \widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_0^\theta$ , observe the following equivalent forms of the recursion

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {k + 1} = \tilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {w}} \widetilde {G} _ {0: k} ^ {(1)} + \tilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k + 1} + \tilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \sum_ {i = 0} ^ {k} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \widetilde {G} _ {i + 1: k} ^ {(1)} \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {i} \\ \Longleftrightarrow \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {k + 1} = (1 - \beta_ {k} a _ {\Delta} / 4) \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {k} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} (\beta_ {k + 1} - \beta_ {k} + \beta_ {k} ^ {2} a _ {\Delta} / 4) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {2} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k} ^ {2} \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {k} \\ \leq (1 - \beta_ {k} a _ {\Delta} / 8) \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {k} + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \beta_ {k} ^ {2} a _ {\Delta} / 4 \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is due to the fact  $\beta_k \widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_2^\theta \leq a_\Delta / 8$ . Subsequently, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {k + 1} \leq \prod_ {i = 0} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {i} a _ {\Delta} / 8) \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {0} + \frac {\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} a _ {\Delta}}{4} \sum_ {j = 0} ^ {k} \gamma_ {j} ^ {2} \prod_ {i = j + 1} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {i} a _ {\Delta} / 8) \\ \leq \prod_ {i = 0} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {i} a _ {\Delta} / 8) \tilde {\mathrm {U}} _ {0} + \frac {\widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} a _ {\Delta}}{4} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 8} \beta_ {k + 1}, \\ \end{array}
$$

Observing that  $\mathrm{M}_{k + 1}^{\tilde{\theta}}\leq \tilde{\mathrm{U}}_{k + 1}$ , we obtain

$$
\boxed {\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {0} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \prod_ {i = 0} ^ {k} (1 - \beta_ {i} a _ {\Delta} / 8) + \widetilde {\mathrm {C}} _ {1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \frac {a _ {\Delta}}{4} \varrho^ {a _ {\Delta} / 8} \beta_ {k + 1},} \tag {79}
$$

We obtain (14) by setting  $\mathrm{C}_1^{\tilde{\theta},\mathrm{mark}} = \frac{a_\Delta}{4}\widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_1^\tilde{\theta}\varrho^{a_\Delta /8}$  and observing  $\widetilde{\mathrm{C}}_0^{\tilde{\theta}}\leq \mathrm{C}_0^{\tilde{\theta},\mathrm{mark}}(1 + \mathrm{V}_0)$  for some constant  $\mathrm{C}_0^{\tilde{\theta},\mathrm{mark}}$

Finally, we bound the tracking error  $\widehat{w}_k \coloneqq w_k - A_{22}^{-1}(b_2 - A_{21}\theta_k)$  as follows. Similarly to the martingale noise case, we set  $\mathrm{M}_k^{\widehat{w}} \coloneqq \|\mathbb{E}[\widehat{w}_k\widehat{w}_k^\top]\|$  and observe:

$$
\mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\widehat {w}} \leq 2 \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + 2 \| L _ {k + 1} \| ^ {2} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}} \leq 2 \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {w}} + 2 \mathrm {L} _ {\infty} ^ {2} \frac {\lambda_ {\max} (Q _ {2 2})}{\lambda_ {\min} (Q _ {\Delta})} \mathrm {M} _ {k + 1} ^ {\tilde {\theta}}
$$

Substituting (79) into (66) gives

$$
